title: Frozen activation selection and selected optional components

The activation selection now validates its persisted pack ID and manifest against the explicit compiled supported entry. It does not re-run Auto to validate an earlier selection. Normal loading and rollback both receive that same frozen selection. A pending activation must still match the user's requested quantization, live mode and effective ceiling; readiness and an inactive custom value do not replace it. Settings comparisons use the actually applied pack when one exists. The saved quantization choice remains Auto or the explicit override as originally requested.

Optional decode forecast files are now an explicit immutable capability of each compiled pack, and Mac setup checks/downloads only the selected pack's list. The original declaration retains its original forecast. No new registry entry or forecast qualification is added. The historical weight manifest serialization remains unchanged; a native identity regression assertion pins that contract. The actual applied arithmetic continues to use the existing engine prefix identity and resource ledger.

Added Mac checks cover persisted Auto selection, exact supported-manifest validation, changed or unavailable identity refusal before creating an activation, explicit-override mismatch, independent readiness and effective-setting comparisons. The existing real missing-model owner now changes readiness while an activation is pending and must still reach ordinary missing-file verification. These checks, native compilation and updated Mac acceptance are pending CI. No local build or model-weight scan overlaps the ongoing frozen held-out campaign. This source preparation does not claim that a second supported pack, its real rollback or Auto hardware profiles have passed.

The earlier native standalone loader commit 6763cd9f1ef7268f6809c4c7a0c1bc6ce29f51da passes complete Mac CI 37225844492, including runtime checks and Xcode build. Its engine coverage and external-library jobs have completed successfully, but the full engine workflow is still running at this checkpoint; no whole-engine pass is asserted here. The standalone allowlist remains empty and actual whole-export/native resource proof is pending.

Raw source and complete Mac CI follow. Home paths are normalized to <HOME>; both original and normalized byte counts and SHA-256 digests are preserved. Compression is checked by roundtrip reconstruction. No model payloads or partial held-out outcomes are included.
